Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in cycles theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 477 and the invariant I(A,B) = 970 mod 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the cycles decomposition. By minimality, the element a^477 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 970-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
